- **multi-omics-integrative-clustering**: Use when you have independent clusterings (or per-dataset kernels) over the same set of observations from M omic platforms and need a single global clustering that down-weights noisy datasets, e.g. pan-cancer subtyping or transcriptional module discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multi-omics-integrative-clustering))
- **multi-omics-integrative-clustering**: Use when you have an exposure matrix, an incomplete omics matrix, and a phenotypic outcome (e.g., prenatal hexachlorobenzene, proteomics with list-wise/sporadic missingness, childhood BMI z-score) and want latent clusters linking them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multi-omics-integrative-clustering))
- **multi-omics-latent-cluster-estimation**: Use when you need to identify latent subgroups jointly defined by an environmental exposure (e.g. maternal hexachlorobenzene), a multi-omics matrix (e.g. proteomics), and a phenotypic outcome (e.g. childhood BMI z-score), including when omics data have missing entries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multi-omics-latent-cluster-estimation))
- **multi-omics-latent-factor-inference**: Use when you have normalized expression/feature matrices for two or more omics modalities measured on the same spatial spots (e.g. spatial-CITE-seq, Stereo-CITE-seq) plus spot coordinates, and need joint dimension reduction that preserves spatial correlation for domain detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multi-omics-latent-factor-inference))
- **multi-omics-latent-space-learning**: Use when you have preprocessed and jointly normalized ST and SM data from the same sample(s) aligned to a common spatial resolution (via joint_adata_sm_st and normalize_total_joint_adata_sm_st), and you need to discover shared latent structure across modalities to identify co-localized spatial. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/multi-omics-latent-space-learning))
- **multi-omics-marker-score-extraction**: Use when Leiden clusters from the post-processed scMoMaT cell-factor KNN graph are available and cluster-specific bio-markers across RNA/ATAC/protein modalities are needed to interpret and annotate clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-omics-marker-score-extraction))
- **multi-omics-method-benchmarking-extraction**: Use when an article compares several mechanistic/pathway modeling tools across fixed property axes (omics data types, Boolean PKN flags such as Ser/Thr Kinome Atlas integration, algorithm class) and you need a machine-readable comparison table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multi-omics-method-benchmarking-extraction))

([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/multi-omics-integration-mixomics-analysis) · ★104 · 2 copies)
